\begin{abstract}Every move below was produced by relational analogy to named master games (human and/or top-engine -- the exact source is named per move). Each figure shows our position with Melanie's move (a) and the top retrieved analog positions (b--d) with the master move played there; the caption names the exact source and the piece-role correspondence under $\mu$ -- cross-type maps (a rook playing a queen's role) are called out, as no material/eval engine explains a move that way.\end{abstract}

\caption{\textbf{Move 7, White: \texttt{Bh4}.} (a) Our position; Melanie plays \texttt{Bh4} (\texttt{g5h4}). Panels (b)--(d) are the retrieved master positions whose correspondence most drove this move (highest QAP objective among those that transfer to it). (b) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0001\#3921} (ply 12) [structural distance $d$=12, QAP obj=117]: the master played \texttt{Bh4}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Bg5} plays the role of that game's \emph{Bg5}; cross-type roles: our \emph{p}a7 as their \emph{R}c1, our \emph{P}f2 as their \emph{B}f1; transferred move \texttt{g5h4} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (c) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0001\#3922} (ply 12) [structural distance $d$=12, QAP obj=117]: the master played \texttt{Bh4}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Bg5} plays the role of that game's \emph{Bg5}; cross-type roles: our \emph{p}a7 as their \emph{R}c1, our \emph{P}f2 as their \emph{B}f1; transferred move \texttt{g5h4} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (d) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0002\#1560} (ply 12) [structural distance $d$=0, QAP obj=110]: the master played \texttt{Bh4}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Bg5} plays the role of that game's \emph{Bg5}; cross-type roles: our \emph{p}e6 as their \emph{R}a1, our \emph{R}a1 as their \emph{P}a2; transferred move \texttt{g5h4} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. Synthesis: 12 retrieved analogs transferred to \texttt{Bh4} (summed QAP objective 1335, the strongest correspondence among the candidate moves); Melanie committed it via the \texttt{search} selector.}
\label{fig:thought-m12}
